def smooth_stats(h,n,tau,S=None): S=compute_S(h); m=S.max(); ex=exp((S-m)/tau); den=ex.sum(); w=ex/den; f=m+tau*log(den); return f,w,S

def grad_weights(h,n,tau,S):
 m=S.max(); ex=exp((S-m)/tau); w=ex/ex.sum(); G=zeros.
 thr=w>1e-5
 for idx in np.where(thr)[0]:
   d=idx-(n-1); wt=w[idx]*dx
   if d==0: G += wt*(1-2h)
   elif d>0: L=n-d; a=h[:L]; b=h[d:]; G[:L]+=wt*(1-b); G[d:]-=wt*a
   else: e=-d; a=h[e:]; b=h[:e]; G[e:]+=wt*(1-b); G[:e]-=wt*a
 return G

Let's develop a concrete algorithm:

Constants:
 target_sum = n/2
 dx = 2/n

Helpers:
- feasible_project(h, target): Euclidean projection as above.
```
def project_feasible(h, target):
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    if abs(h.sum()-target) < 1e-14: return h
    lo, hi = -1.0, 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid=(lo+hi)/2
        s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
        if s < target: lo=mid
        else: hi=mid
    return np.clip(h+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
```
This projection changes mean but may not be best if sum far? It is exact nearest under shift. But if h not feasible sum, okay. The result feasible. Need exact target maybe floating error; after one final adjust:
```
val=np.clip(...); diff=target-val.sum(); free=(val<1 - eps) if diff>0 else (val>eps); val[free]+=diff/free.sum(); return clip
```
But binary search sum monotone; use 60 iters; result within 1e-12. Good.

def target_for(n): return n/2.0

- grad_from_weights(h,n,w): Use loop over idx,w_i. Could optimize by only idx where w_i > 1e-8. Since sum w=1. For each idx. Use dx factor. We'll not multiply dx inside? Let's set G in C units: G += (w* dx)*...
```
def grad_weights(h,n,w, dx):
    G=np.zeros(n)
    center=n-1
    for k in range(len(w)):
       wt=w[k]*dx
       if wt < 1e-12: continue
       d=k-center
       if d==0: G += wt*(1-2*h)
       elif d>0:
          L=n-d
          G[:L] += wt*(1-h[d:])
          G[d:] -= wt*h[:L]
       else:
          e=-d
          G[e:] += wt*(1-h[:e])
          G[:e] -= wt*h[e:]
    return G
```
Careful negative: term h[e:] (1-h[:e]); first F=h[e:], second B=h[:e]; derivative first:1-B; second:-F. Good.

If w from logsumexp normalized, G is gradient of f (C units). Good.

def gradient_polish(h,n,deadline,rng,tau_start,tau_end,steps=?):
    h=project_feasible(h,n/2)
    S=compute_S; cur=S.max()*dx; best=cur; best_h=h.copy(); tau=tau_start
    no=0
    while time.time()<deadline:
       w,m=weights(S,tau)
       G=grad_weights(h,n,w,dx)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(G)
       if gnorm<1e-10: break
       base_alpha = min(1.0, 0.05/gnorm) # step target 0.05
       accepted=False
       for mult in [1,2,4,0.5,0.25,0.12]:
           a=base_alpha*mult
           cand=project_feasible(h - a*G, n/2)
           S_c=compute_S(cand,n); cc=S_c.max()*dx
           if cc < cur - 1e-12:
               h=cand; S=S_c; cur=cc; accepted=True; break
           # if smooth? maybe no
       if accepted:
          if cur<best-1e-14: best=cur; best_h=h.copy(); no=0
          # reduce tau if many improvements?
       else:
          no+=1
          if no>10:
             tau = max(tau*0.5, tau_end*0.5)
             # perturb
             h = project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0, 0.02*max(tau,0.1), n), n/2)
             S=compute_S(h,n); cur=max*?
             no=0
          if tau<tau_end: tau=max(tau*0.95,tau_end)
    return best_h,best
```

```
def h_anneal(h,n,deadline,rng,tau_seq):
  h=project; cur=...; best=...; temp=0.5? in S units (C=dx* )
  while time<deadline:
    S=compute; w=...
    G=grad
    if norm small: reduce tau/restart
    for step mult:
      cand=project(h-aG)
      cnew=...
      if cnew < cur - 1e-12:
         accept; if cnew<best: best; cur=cnew
         temp=max(0, temp*0.999)
         break
    else no improve
      # perturb with temp
      if temp > 1e-5:
         # allow random move that not too bad
         cand=project(h + rng.normal(0,sigma,n)); c=...
         if c < cur + temp: accept (cur=c; temp*=0.9) maybe
      reduce tau
      if stuck: h=best + noise
```

Detailed polish algorithm:

```
def polish(h, n, deadline, rng, tau_start=8.0, tau_end=0.05):
    target=n/2
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    S=compute_S(h,n)
    cur=S.max()*(2/n)
    best=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    tau=tau_start
    no_improve=0
    max_no=20
    while time.time() < deadline:
        S=compute_S(h,n)
        m, w = smooth_weights(S, tau)
        fcur = dx*(m + tau*np.log(w.sum()?)) # w sum 1, f=m+tau*log(sumexp?) If w already normalized, sumexp/e^m; f=m+tau*log(sum_exp_m0). Need return f.
        G=grad_weights(h,n,w,dx)
        gnorm=np.linalg.norm(G)
        if gnorm < 1e-10: break
        # step size base: target change norm maybe 0.12
        base = min(1.0, 0.12 / gnorm, 1e4)
        accepted=False
        for mult in (1.0, 2.0, 0.5, 0.25, 4.0, 0.125):
            a = base*mult
            cand = project_feasible(h - a*G, n)
            Sc = compute_S(cand,n)
            c = Sc.max()*dx
            # also smooth candidate? compute info
            wc, fc = smooth_weights(Sc, tau)?? returns w,f. Need no w? f.
            if c < cur - 1e-13 or (fc < fcur - 1e-10 and c <= cur + 1e-7):
                h = cand; S=Sc; cur=c
                accepted=True
                if cur < best - 1e-14:
                    best=cur; best_h=h.copy(); no_improve=0
                break
        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 5:
                tau = max(tau_end, tau*0.7)
            if no_improve > max_no:
                # perturb around best
                sigma = 0.01 * max(1.0, np.sqrt(tau))
                h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0, sigma, n), n)
                S=compute_S(h,n); cur=S.max()*dx
                no_improve=0
                tau = min(tau_start*0.5, tau*1.5)
            else:
                # small noise to current to break plateau
                h = project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0, 0.003, n), n)
                S=compute_S(h,n); cur=S.max()*dx
                if cur > best + 0.01:
                    h=best_h.copy()
        # after accepted, cool tau if stuck? Maybe reduce after some accepts
        # Track iter count: if no for 10, reduce tau; else after 200 reduces.
    return best_h, best
```

Implement final `ensure_feasible`:
```
def ensure_feasible(h,n):
   target=n/2.0
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-14: return h
   if diff>0: idx=np.where(h<1-1e-12)[0]
   else: idx=np.where(h>1e-12)[0]
   h[idx]+=diff/len(idx)
   return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
Maybe not exact but close.

Implement:
```
def project_feasible(v,n):
    target=n/2.0
    v=np.clip(v,0.0,1.0)
    # quick if close
    if abs(v.sum()-target)<1e-12: return v
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    # initial s maybe at v (λ=0), shift bounds ensure target reachable
    for _ in range(45):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(v+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    v=np.clip(v+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # final adjust one free
    diff=target-v.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       idx=np.where((v>1e-10)&(v<1-1e-10))[0]
       if idx.size:
          k=idx[0]; add=diff
          if 0<=v[k]+add<=1: v[k]+=add
          else:
             # distribute a few
             m=(v>1e-10)&(v<1-1e-10); v[m]+=diff/m.sum(); v=clip
    return v
```

But if v has values far, λ within [-1,1]. Fine. Use 40 iters enough: interval 2/2^40~1.8e-12; sum error <= n*eps? Good. 40*250=10k ops per projection. If 30k projections ->300M, okay maybe. Let's write `make_weights` with logsumexp:
```
def make_weights(S,tau,active_tol):
    m=float(S.max())
    if active_tol is not None:
       w=(S >= m-active_tol).astype(float)
       s=w.sum()
       if s==0: w[S.argmax()]=1
       else: w/=s
       return w,m
    s=S-m
    emax=np.exp(s/tau)
    den=emax.sum()
    if den<=0: w=zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else w=emax/den
    f=m+tau*np.log(den)
    return w,m,f? We need f. Return w,f.
```
For gradient, need w only; for current f, need f. Could compute f = (m + tau*np.log(den)) * dx. But for line search candidate we need f. Function `smooth_info(S,tau,active_tol)` returns w,f (C units):

else: return w, (m+tau*log(den))*dx
```

Pseudo polish:
```
def polish(h,n,timeout,rng,tau=4.0):
  deadline=time.time()+timeout
  h=project; cur=final; best=cur; best_h=h
  temp=0.01  # C units
  while time<deadline:
    S=compute
    w,fcur=smooth_info(S,tau,active_tol=None if tau>0.1 else 0.1)
    G=grad
    if norm(G)>eps:
      line search; if accept:
        cur=cn; if cur<best: best...
        temp=max(temp*0.999,1e-5)
        continue
    # no gradient accept
    # random perturbation
    sigma = min(0.05, 0.01 + 0.02*sqrt(tau)/? )
    cand=project(h + rng.normal(0,sigma,n))
    c=final
    if c < best + temp or c < cur + temp:
       h=cand; cur=c; temp*=0.9
    else:
       # try pair move from best
       if pair_move improves: update best/current
    # reduce tau sometimes
    tau=max(0.05,tau*0.9)
    if cur > best + temp*2: h=best_h.copy()
  return best_h,best
```

def gradient_optimize(h,n,deadline,rng,tau0=10.0,tau_min=0.0):
   target=n/2
   h=project; cur=...; best=cur; best_h=h
   tau=tau0; stall=0; total_step=0
   while time.time()<deadline:
      S=compute; fcur,w=smooth_info(S,tau,tau_min? if tau==0 active)
      G=grad_weights(...); gnorm=norm(G)
      accepted=False
      if gnorm > 1e-10:
         base=min(1.0, 0.2/gnorm)
         for mult in [1,2,4,0.5,0.25,0.125]:
            a=base*mult
            cand=project(h - a*G)
            Sc=compute; c=Sc.max()*dx

         if (total_step % 200 ==0) or stall? tau = max(tau_min, tau*0.99)

            h=project(best_h + rng.normal(0,sigma,n))
            cur=final; S=compute
            stall=0
         # if tau==tau_min and stuck, active_tol vary
      if tau==0? Use active.
   return best

def improve(h, n, seconds, rng):
    end = time.time()+seconds
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    cur=final; best=cur; best_h=h
    tau=8.0
    stall=0
    pend=0
    while time.time()<end:
        S=compute
        if tau < 0.05:
            w,f = active_info(S, active_tol=0.2 if tau>=0 else 0.1)
        else:
            w,f = log_info(S,tau)
        G=grad
        gnorm=norm
        accepted=False
        if gnorm>1e-10:
            base = min(1.0, 0.15/gnorm)
            # try larger first if not many active? maybe [1,2,0.5,4,0.25]
            for mult in (1.0,2.0,0.5,4.0,0.25,0.125):
                cand=project(h - base*mult*G)
                Sc=compute; c=Sc.max()*dx
                wc,fc=info(Sc,...)
                if (c < cur - 1e-13) or (fc < f - 1e-10 and c < f + 1e-6):
                    h=cand; cur=c; accepted=True
                    if c < best: best=c; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
                    break
        if accepted:
            pend += 1
            if pend % 200 ==0:
                tau = max(0.02, tau*0.98)
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall == 5:
                tau = max(0.02, tau*0.5)
                # maybe increase active_tol by setting tau negative? use variable active_tol
            if stall > 20:
                # random restart around best
                sigma = 0.01 if tau<1 else 0.02
                h = project(best_h + rng.normal(0,sigma,n))
                cur = final
                if cur > best + 0.02:
                   h = best_h.copy(); cur=best
                stall=0
                tau = max(1.0, min(tau*1.5, 10.0))  # broaden
        # if using active and stuck, maybe vary active_tol: incorporate in stall reset? Could just tau.
    return best_h,best
```

def pair_step(h,n,rng):
   S=compute; cur=S.max()*dx
   G=grad with active weights? Use current active uniform: m=S.max(); act=S>=m-0.2; w=act/sum.
   G=grad_weights

   j = np.argmax(G) (decrease)
   if i==j return None
   best_t=0; best_c=cur
   # t direction transfer from j to i: max_t = min(h[i]?, actually increase i limited 1-h[i], decrease j limited h[j])

def proj(h,n): ... uses target=n/2
def S_of(h,n): return np.correlate(h,1-h,'full').astype(float)

def final_c(h,n): return c_of(S_of(h,n),n)

def smooth_info(S,tau,active_tol):

      w = (S >= m-active_tol).astype(float)
      s=w.sum()
      if s==0:
         w[S.argmax()]=1
      else: w/=s
      return w, m*(2.0/S.size? no n) ??? Need dx. We don't have n. Pass dx.

`improve_cont(h,n,seconds,rng)`:
```
def improve_cont(start,n,seconds,rng):
   end=time.time()+seconds
   h=proj(start,n); cur=final_c; best=cur; best_h=h.copy()
   tau=8.0
   stall=0; accepts=0; t_phase=0
   while time.time()<end:
      S=compute(h,n)
      if tau < 0.06:
         active = 0.2 if tau>=0.03 else 0.1
         w,f=smooth_info(S,n,tau,active)
      else:
         w,f=smooth_info(S,n,tau,None)
      G=grad(h,n,w)
      gn=np.linalg.norm(G)
      acc=False
      if gn>1e-10:
         base=min(1.0,0.18/gn,1e6)
         for mult in (1.0,2.0,0.5,4.0,0.25,0.125):
             a=base*mult
             cand=proj(h-a*G,n)
             Sc=compute(cand,n); cc=c_of(Sc,n)
             wc,fc=smooth_info(Sc,n,tau,active if active? need same)
             if cc < cur - 1e-12 or (fc < f - 1e-10 and cc < max(cur,best)+0.008):
                 h=cand; cur=cc; acc=True
                 if cc < best - 1e-13: best=cc; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
                 break
      if acc:
         accepts+=1
         if accepts % 100 ==0:
            tau=max(0.04,tau*0.95)
      else:
         stall+=1
         if stall==6:
            tau=max(0.04,tau*0.55)
         if stall>18:
            sigma=0.01+0.01*min(1.0,tau/4.0)
            h=proj(best_h + rng.normal(0,sigma,n),n)
            cur=final_c(h,n)
            if cur > best + 0.02:
               h=best_h.copy(); cur=best
            stall=0
            if tau<1.0: tau=1.0
   return proj(best_h,n), best
```

def pair_move(h,n,rng):

   tol=0.25
   act=S>=m-tol
   w=act/act.sum(); if sum0...

   # direction: increase argmin G, decrease argmax G
   inc=np.argmin(G); dec=np.argmax(G)
   if inc==dec: return None
   best_c=cur; best_h=None
   tmax = min(1-h[inc], h[dec])
   if tmax <= 1e-8: swap? return None

       nh=h.copy(); nh[inc]+=t; nh[dec]-=t

   # reverse: inc2=argmax? Actually try transfer from inc to dec maybe

def binary_round(h,n):
   b=np.zeros(n); idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]; b[idx[:n//2]]=1; return b
def binary_local(h,n,seconds,rng):
   end=...; b=round; S=...; cur=c; best=...
   ones=np.where(b==1)[0]; zeros=...
   stall=0
   while time<end:
      S=compute; m=S.max(); cur=m*dx
      if cur<best: best=cur; best_h=b.copy(); stall=0
      # active bad pairs:
      active = np.where(S >= m - 1.0)[0]
      cand_set=[]
      for k in active[:20]:
         d=k-(n-1)
         if d>0:
            L=n-d; bad = (b[:L]==1)&(b[d:]==0); idx=np.nonzero(bad)[0]
            # sample up to 10 bad pairs: zero=i=d+idx, one=j=idx
         elif d<0:
            e=-d; bad=(b[e:]==1)&(b[:e]==0); i=e+idx; j=idx
         # for each candidate zero=i, one=j, propose swap
      # Also random 30 swaps
      # Evaluate candidates with full recompute until improved or limited
      found=False
      for zero,one in candidates (max 300):
         nb=b.copy(); nb[zero]=1; nb[one]=0; c=final_c(nb,n)
         if c<cur: accept; found=True; break
      if not found:
         # random perturbation: do 5 random accepted? or add random 10 swaps if improves best+temp
         for _ in range(20): random swap; if c < best + 0.02: accept current
         stall+=1
         if stall>30: reset to best
   return best_h,best

def initial_candidates(h0,n,rng):
   xs=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
   cs=[]
   if h0 is not None:
      h=proj(h0,n); c=final_c; cs.append((h,c))
   def add(h): h=proj(h,n); c=final_c; cs.append((h,c))
   # one mode grid
   for sign in (1,-1):
      for a in np.linspace(0.0,0.5,21):
         h=0.5 + sign*a*np.cos(np.pi*xs)
         add(h)
   # two/base Fourier random
   M=6; scales=np.array([0.25,0.15,0.08,0.05,0.03,0.02])
   B=np.column_stack([np.cos(m*np.pi*xs) for m in range(1,M+1)])
   for _ in range(30):
      coeff=rng.normal(0,scales)
      h=0.5 + B.dot(coeff)
      # scale if maxclip large? maybe h=0.5+0.5*np.tanh(2*(...)) for bounds; but clip
      add(h)
   # smooth random
   r=rng.normal(0,0.1,n); kernel=np.ones(5)/5; h=0.5+np.convolve(kernel,r,mode='same'); add(h)
   # Deduplicate by c? Keep top 6 by c
   cs.sort(key=lambda x:x[1])
   top=[]; seen maybe not
   for h,c in cs:
       if len(top)>=8: break
       top.append((h,c))
   return top
```
Cost of add 21*2+30+1 ~75, each compute_S. Good.

```
def improve_cont(start, n, seconds, rng, tau_start=6.0, tau_min=0.02):
    end = time.time() + seconds
    target = n/2.0
    h = proj(start,n)
    S = S_of(h,n); cur = c_of(S,n); best=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    tau = tau_start
    stall=0; accepts=0
    while time.time() < end:
        S = S_of(h,n)
        if tau < 0.08:
            at = 0.15 if tau >= 0.03 else 0.08
            w, f = smooth_info(S,n,tau,at)
        else:
            w, f = smooth_info(S,n,tau,None)
            at = None
        G = grad_weights(h,n,w)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(G))
        acc = False
        if gn > 1e-11:
            base = min(1.0, 0.25 / gn, 1.0e6)
            # If tau large, use a bit larger steps? maybe base*mult as below
            for mult in (1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 0.5, 3.0, 0.25):
                a = base * mult
                if a <= 0: continue
                cand = proj(h - a*G, n)
                Sc = S_of(cand,n)
                cc = c_of(Sc,n)
                if cc < best - 1e-13:  # immediate global? no
                    pass
                if cc < cur - 1e-12:
                    h=cand; cur=cc; acc=True
                    if cc < best - 1e-13:
                        best=cc; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
                    break
                if at is not None:
                    # exact active monotonic? skip smooth? no
                else:
                    wc, fc = smooth_info(Sc,n,tau,None)
                    if fc < f - 1e-10 and cc < max(cur,best) + 0.01:
                        h=cand; cur=cc; acc=True
                        if cc < best - 1e-13:
                            best=cc; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
                        break
        if acc:
            accepts += 1
            if accepts % 80 == 0:
                tau = max(tau_min, tau * 0.97)
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall == 5:
                tau = max(tau_min, tau * 0.6)
            if stall > 25:
                # pair move from best
                pm = pair_move(best_h,n,rng)
                if pm is not None and pm[1] < best - 1e-13:
                    best=pm[1]; best_h=proj(pm[0],n); h=best_h.copy(); cur=best; stall=0
                else:
                    sig = 0.008 + 0.012*min(1.0, tau/3.0)
                    h = proj(best_h + rng.normal(0, sig, n), n)
                    cur = c_of(S_of(h,n), n)
                    if cur > best + 0.015:
                        h = best_h.copy(); cur = best
                    stall=0
                    tau = max(tau, 0.5)
    return proj(best_h,n), min(best, c_of(S_of(best_h,n),n))
```

Pair move details:
```
def pair_move(h,n,rng):
   S=S_of(h,n); m=S.max(); cur=c_of(S,n)
   tol=0.25
   act = S >= m - tol
   cnt=act.sum()
   if cnt==0: act[S.argmax()]=1
   w=act/ cnt
   G=grad_weights(h,n,w)
   best_h=None; best_c=cur
   for inc_i,dec_j in ((int(np.argmin(G)), int(np.argmax(G))), (int(np.argmax(G)), int(np.argmin(G)))):
       if inc_i==dec_j: continue
       tmax=min(1-h[inc_i], h[dec_j])
       if tmax <=1e-8: continue
       for frac in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.005,0.33):
          t=frac*tmax
          nh=h.copy(); nh[inc_i]+=t; nh[dec_j]-=t
          c=c_of(S_of(nh,n),n)
          if c < best_c - 1e-14:
             best_c=c; best_h=nh
   if best_h is not None: return proj(best_h,n), best_c
   return None
```
The direction `inc` should be value to increase; if argmin G (gradient negative) yes increase reduces. `dec` argmax G decrease. Reverse may not make sense but okay.
Cost ~14 compute_S. Call rarely.

Binary local maybe implement after continuous final if time. Let's write compact:
```
def binary_local(h0,n,seconds,rng):
   end=time.time()+seconds
   b=bin_round(h0,n)
   S=S_of(b,n); cur=c_of(S,n); best=cur; best_b=b.copy()
   # local with active bad pairs
   while time.time()<end:
      S=S_of(b,n); m=S.max(); cur=c_of(S,n)
      if cur < best: best=cur; best_b=b.copy()
      active=np.where(S >= m - 1.0)[0]
      tries=0; improved=False
      # active candidate swaps, sample up to 80
      cand=[]
      for k in active:
         d=k-(n-1)
         if d>0:
            L=n-d
            mask=(b[:L]==1) & (b[d:]==0)
            idx=np.nonzero(mask)[0]
            for p in idx[:5]: cand.append((d+p,p)) # zero, one
         elif d<0:
            e=-d
            mask=(b[e:]==1) & (b[:e]==0)
            idx=np.nonzero(mask)[0]
            for p in idx[:5]: cand.append((e+p,p))
      # add random swaps
      if len(cand) < 20:
         ones=np.where(b==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(b==0)[0]
         for _ in range(20): cnd=(rng.choice(zeros), rng.choice(ones)); cand.append(cnd)
      # shuffle candidates
      rng.shuffle(cand)
      for zero,one in cand:
         if zero==one: continue
         mb=b.copy(); mb[zero]=1; mb[one]=0
         c=c_of(S_of(mb,n),n)
         if c < cur - 1e-12:
             b=mb; cur=c; improved=True; break
      if improved: continue
      # if no active, try random accepted worse to escape
      for _ in range(10):
         i=rng.choice(np.where(b==0)[0]); j=rng.choice(np.where(b==1)[0])
         mb=b.copy(); mb[i]=1; mb[j]=0
         c=c_of(S_of(mb,n),n)
         if c < best + 0.02:
             b=mb; cur=c; break
      # if stuck long? no counter? Add stall
   return best_b,best
```

Let's ensure `grad_weights` handles n and w length =2n-1. For negative d, code:
```
e = -d
G[e:] += wt*(1 - h[:e])

```
Check derivative for negative lag d=-e: S=∑_{r=0}^{n-e-1} h[r+e]*(1-h[r]). Let F=h[e:], B=h[:e]. Gradient: for first F positions e: +=1 - B; for second B positions 0:e: -=F. Good.

For d>0: F=h[:L], B=h[d:]. Good.

For d=0: S=∑h(1-h). derivative =1-2h. Good.

Now, maybe `np.correlate` lag order: For positive `k` index n-1+d, we assumed term h[i](1-h[i+d]). Let's verify with small n=2: h=[a,b], 1-h=[1-a,1-b]. correlate full: c[0]=a*(1-b)? Actually numpy correlate(a,b,full): result[k] = ∑_i a[i] b[i+k-(n-1)]? Let's test mentally: For n=2, full length3. Standard correlation: c[0]=h[0]*(1-h)[1] maybe? c[1]=h[0]*(1-h)[0]+h[1]*(1-h)[1]; c[2]=h[1]*(1-h)[0]. Gradient indexing must match actual S index. Need correct mapping.

Definition of objective: max over k of ∫ h(x)(1-h(x+k)) dx. In discrete, for shift +d>0: term h_i(1-h_{i+d}). Which correlate index? ```
def S_of(h,n):
    S=np.empty(2*n-1)
    C=n-1
    for d in range(-n+1,n):
       if d==0: S[C]=np.dot(h,1-h)
       elif d>0:
          L=n-d; S[C+d]=np.dot(h[:L], 1-h[d:])
       else:
          e=-d; L=n-e; S[C+d]=np.dot(h[e:], 1-h[:e])
    return S
```

Potential bug in `project_feasible`: Using binary search λ with `v+mid`. If v contains values far outside [0,1]? We call after clipping. Binary search with `np.clip(v+mid,0,1).sum()`. We set lo=-1,hi=1 because at -1 sum 0, at 1 sum n. But if v has nan? no. Use 36 iterations. Then final adjust.

Performance of `project_feasible` called millions? We can optimize by checking sum close before binary.
```
def project_feasible(v,n):
    target=n/2.0
    v=np.clip(v,0.0,1.0)
    s=v.sum()
    if abs(s-target) < 1e-13: return v
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    for _ in range(36):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.clip(v+mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    v=np.clip(v+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-v.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       free=(v>1e-12)&(v<1.0-1e-12)
       if free.any(): v[free] += diff/free.sum()
       v=np.clip(v,0.0,1.0)
    return v
```
If diff large and free sum not enough, maybe one adjustment over/under; but free includes all nonbounds, diff should be small after binary within 1e-11*250 ~2.5e-9, so fine.

In binary search, target exactly n/2 maybe .? n even integer or .5. Good.

`smooth_info`: threshold for weights. If tau very small, ex for max may underflow for others; `den` ~ count active. If all underflow? max term exp(0)=1 so den>=1. Good.
```
def smooth_info(S,n,tau,active_tol):
   m=float(S.max())
   dx=2.0/n
   if active_tol is not None:
       w=(S >= m-active_tol).astype(float)
       s=w.sum()
       if s==0:
           w=np.zeros_like(S); w[int(np.argmax(S))]=1.0
       else: w=w/s
       return w, m*dx
   sh=(S-m)/tau
   ex=np.exp(sh)
   den=ex.sum()
   if den <= 1e-300:
       w=zeros; w[argmax]=1
       return w, m*dx
   w=ex/den
   return w, (m + tau*math.log(den))*dx
```
Need import math for log. Or use np.log. Use math.

`grad_weights` modifies G slices; Gaussian? We need ensure not aliasing issues when using views: `G[:L] += wt*(1-h[d:])` and `G[d:] -= wt*h[:L]`. If L and d overlap? For n=2, L=1,d=1 no overlap. For d large, slices may overlap? Example d=0 handled separately. For d>0, G[:L] and G[d:] overlap if d < L, yes e.g., n=250,d=10,L=240, overlap indices 10..239. Doing two in-place augmented assignments overlapping is problematic because first statement changes G, second subtracts using h (not G) so no dependence on G? It subtracts from G[d:], which may include elements just added in G[:L]. That's correct: a variable can appear as first in some pairs and second in same lag? For d>0, S=∑ h_i(1-h_{i+d}). Variable h_p appears as first for i=p (term derivative 1-h_{p+d}) and as second for i=p-d (deriv -h_{p-d}). So G_p should receive both additions from first and second for same lag. In-place sequential slice updates: First add first contributions to all p<L. Then subtract second contributions from p>=d. For p in overlap [d,L-1], it receives addition from first (if p<L) and subtraction from second (if p>=d). Since subtraction uses `G[d:] -= wt*h[:L]`, it subtracts from G that already includes addition; order okay because operations independent (add then subtract). But `G[d:]` view overlaps with G[:L], but NumPy handles in-place with RHS precomputed `wt*h[:L]`; updates each element in view once; no issue. Good.

For d<0, similar. For d=0 add `1-2h`.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    hard_end=t0 + max(10, budget_s - 8.0)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try: h0=...; n=len(h0)
    except: n=250; h0=0.5*np.ones(n)
    if n < 2: n=250; h0=0.5*np.ones(n)
    # If n odd? Keep; if n <10? no.
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n)
    c0=final_c(h0,n)
    best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=c0
    collection=[]; ctr=0
    # add candidates
    def? no local. Just repeated code using add_candidate.
    ctr=add_candidate(collection,h0,n,ctr)
    # noisy parent
    for sig in (0.01,0.03,0.08):
       ctr=add_candidate(collection,h0+rng.normal(0,sig,n),n,ctr)
    # one-mode
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    c1=np.cos(np.pi*x)
    for sign in (1.0,-1.0):
       for a in np.linspace(0.0,0.5,13):
          h=0.5+sign*a*c1
          ctr=add_candidate(collection,h,n,ctr)
    # random Fourier
    M=6; scales=np.array([0.25,0.15,0.08,0.05,0.03,0.02])
    B=np.empty((n, M))
    for m in range(1,M+1): B[:,m-1]=np.cos(m*np.pi*x)
    for _ in range(30):
       coeff=rng.normal(0.0, scales[:M])
       h=0.5 + B.dot(coeff)
       ctr=add_candidate(collection,h,n,ctr)
    # smooth random
    kernel=np.array([1,2,3,2,1], float); kernel/=9
    for _ in range(6):
       h=0.5 + np.convolve(kernel, rng.normal(0,0.08,n), mode='same')
       ctr=add_candidate(collection,h,n,ctr)
    collection.sort()
    cands=[]
    for item in collection:
       if len(cands)>=6: break
       cands.append(item[2])  # item (c,ctr,h)
    usable=hard_end - time.time()
    if usable < 30: return best_h,best_c,n
    # weights for nonfinal, top candidates excluding first? Let's just process cands with weights relative.
    weights=np.ones(min(5,len(cands))); weights[0]=1.2? But if cands[0] parent and c0 best. Let top up to5.
    # allocate 0.62 usable to candidates, 0.38 final.
    nonfinal=0.60*usable

       w=np.ones(len(cands)); w[0]=1.5; w[1:]*=0.7? Ensure not too much.

Let's write `improve_cont` carefully:
```
def improve_cont(start,n,seconds,rng,tau_start=6.0,tau_min=0.02):
    if seconds <= 0: return project_feasible(start,n), final_c(start,n)
    end=time.time()+seconds
    h=project_feasible(start,n)
    S=S_of(h,n); cur=c_of(S,n); best=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    tau=tau_start; stall=0; accepts=0
    while time.time() < end:
        S=S_of(h,n)
        cur=c_of(S,n)
        if cur < best - 1e-13:
            best=cur; best_h=h.copy()
        if cur > best + 0.02:
            h=best_h.copy(); S=S_of(h,n); cur=best
        active=None
        if tau < 0.08:
           active=0.15 if tau >= 0.03 else 0.08
           w,f=smooth_info(S,n,tau,active)
        else:
           w,f=smooth_info(S,n,tau,None)
        G=grad_weights(h,n,w)
        gn=float(np.linalg.norm(G))
        acc=False
        if gn > 1e-11:
           base=min(1.0, 0.25/gn, 1e6)
           for mult in (1.0,0.5,2.0,0.25,4.0,0.125):
               a=base*mult
               if a <= 0: continue
               cand=project_feasible(h - a*G, n)
               Sc=S_of(cand,n); cc=c_of(Sc,n)
               if cc < cur - 1e-12:
                   h=cand; cur=cc; acc=True
                   if cc < best - 1e-13:
                       best=cc; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
                   break
               if active is None and cc < max(cur,best) + 0.01:
                   wc,fc=smooth_info(Sc,n,tau,None)
                   if fc < f - 1e-10:
                       h=cand; cur=cc; acc=True
                       if cc < best - 1e-13:
                           best=cc; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
                       break
        if acc:
            accepts +=1
            if accepts % 80 ==0:
                tau=max(tau_min, tau*0.97)
        else:
            stall +=1
            if stall == 5:
                tau=max(tau_min, tau*0.6)
            if stall >= 40 and stall % 10 ==0:
                pm=pair_move(best_h,n,rng)
                if pm is not None and pm[1] < best - 1e-13:
                    best=pm[1]; best_h=project_feasible(pm[0],n); h=best_h.copy(); cur=best; stall=0
            if stall >= 60:
                sigma=0.008 + 0.012*min(1.0, max(tau,0.0)/3.0)
                h=project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0, sigma, n), n)
                cur=c_of(S_of(h,n),n)
                if cur > best + 0.02:
                    h=best_h.copy(); cur=best
                stall=0
                tau=max(tau, 0.5)
    return project_feasible(best_h,n), min(best, final_c(best_h,n))
```
In smooth condition, compute `smooth_info(Sc,n,tau,None)` even if tau maybe <0.08? active not None, skip. If tau large, okay. Could compute fc before cc? We need cc first. Fine.
Potential issue: If `cc` is much larger than best but `fc<f` and `cc < max(cur,best)+0.01`, if cur already best+0.01, allow best+0.02. At loop start reset if cur>best+0.02. So current can drift at most 0.02. Good.

`pair_move` uses `best_h` and `n`. It might call S_of and grad_weights with active weights. It uses `argmin/argmax`; if all G zero returns None? It will still try pair directions. If inc=dec maybe both? G zero, argmin=0 argmax=0, skip. Good.

Let's review binary local candidate active mapping with correlate and d=C-k. Use if n even. Code:
```
def binary_local(start,n,seconds,rng):
   if seconds<=0: return binary_round(start,n), final_c(binary_round(start,n),n)
   end=time.time()+seconds
   b=binary_round(start,n)
   S=S_of(b,n); cur=c_of(S,n); best=cur; bb=b.copy(); stall=0; dx=2.0/n
   while time.time()<end:
      S=S_of(b,n); m=float(S.max()); cur=m*dx
      if cur < best - 1e-12:
         best=cur; bb=b.copy(); stall=0
      C=n-1
      active=np.where(S >= m - 1.0)[0]
      cand=[]; seen=set()
      for k in active:
         d=C-k
         if d>0:
            L=n-d; base=d
            if L<=0: continue
            mask=(b[:L]==1.0)&(b[base:]==0.0)
            idxs=np.nonzero(mask)[0]
            for p in idxs[:5]:
               z=base+p; o=p

```
def project_feasible(v,n):
    target=n/2
    v=np.clip(v,0,1)
    diff=target-v.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-12: return v
    # try distribute to free few iterations
    for _ in range(5):
       if diff>0: mask=v<1.0
       else: mask=v>0.0
       c=mask.sum()
       if c==0: break
       add=diff/c
       v[mask]+=add
       v=np.clip(v,0,1)
       diff=target-v.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
    if abs(diff)>1e-11:
       binary fallback
    return v
```
This avoids binary search for common small diff. Distribution equal to all free may saturate if diff large, but 5 iterations; fallback for large. Compute mask and diff. This may be faster. However distributing equally to all nonbounds may alter more values than L2 projection but acceptable. It preserves sum. For small step, one iteration enough. Let's use this faster projection. Ensure exact sum: after distribution, due floating diff maybe 1e-14; if >1e-10 fallback binary. Fallback uses 36 iters rarely. This will speed greatly.

Implement:
```
def project_feasible(v,n):
    target=n/2.0
    v=np.clip(v,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(8):
        diff=target-v.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-14: return v
        if diff>0:
            mask=v < 1.0 - 1e-15
        else:
            mask=v > 1e-15
        if not mask.any(): break
        v[mask] += diff / mask.sum()
        v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
    # fallback binary shift
    diff=target-v.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-11:
        lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
        for _ in range(40):
           mid=(lo+hi)/2
           s=np.clip(v+mid,0,1).sum()
           if s < target: lo=mid
           else: hi=mid
        v=np.clip(v+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    diff=target-v.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
        free=(v>1e-12)&(v<1-1e-12)
        if free.any():
            v[free] += diff/free.sum()
            v=np.clip(v,0,1)
    return v
```
The iterative equal distribution can over-saturate many in one go if diff large, but then clip and next iteration distributes among remaining. If all saturate before sum reached? If target within [0,n], after enough iterations should. The mask uses all non-saturated; adding diff/count may push many over 1, clipped. Next diff smaller. 8 iterations maybe enough for small; fallback otherwise. Good.

Potential issue: This projection not Euclidean nearest; may change values differently, affecting line search. But likely okay. For rigorous, fallback binary if diff large. For small, distributing equally is close to shift for unsaturated. Good.

Now, final exact sum: The loop returns v with diff <1e-14 if good. The final free distribution may leave diff tiny. Good.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    hard_end = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        n = 250

    collection=[]; ctr=0
    ctr = add_candidate(collection, h0, n, ctr)
    for sig in (0.01, 0.03, 0.08):
       ctr = add_candidate(collection, h0 + rng.normal(0.0, sig, n), n, ctr)
    x = np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    c1 = np.cos(np.pi*x)
    c2 = np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x)
    for a1 in np.linspace(-0.5,0.5,11):
       for a2 in np.linspace(-0.3,0.3,7):
          h = 0.5 + a1*c1 + a2*c2
          ctr = add_candidate(collection, h, n, ctr)
    # random fourier
    M=6; scales=...; B=...
    for _ in range(40): ...
    # smooth random
    ...
    collection.sort()

    for c, id, h in collection: # tuple is (c,counter,h); unpack works: c float, id int, h array
       cands.append(h)
       if len(cands)>=6: break
    usable=hard_end-time.time()
    if usable < 25:
       return best_h,best_c,n
    weights = np.ones(min(5, len(cands)))
    if len(weights)==0: return...
    weights[0]=1.4
    if len(weights)>1: weights[1]=0.9
    if len(weights)>2: weights[2]=0.7
    if len(weights)>3: weights[3]=0.5
    if len(weights)>4: weights[4]=0.4
    nonfinal=0.60*usable
    wsum=float(weights.sum())
    for idx in range(len(weights)):
       if time.time() >= hard_end - 0.34*usable: break
       sec=nonfinal*(weights[idx]/wsum)
       if sec < 8.0: sec=8.0
       hh,cc = improve_cont(cands[idx], n, sec, rng, 6.0, 0.02)
       if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
           best_c=cc; best_h=hh
    rem=hard_end-time.time()
    if n %2 ==0 and rem > 150:
       sec = min(70.0, rem - 80.0)
       if sec > 10:
          bb,cb=binary_local(best_h,n,sec,rng)
          if cb < best_c -1e-14:
              best_c=cb; best_h=bb
          rem=hard_end-time.time()
    if rem > 25:
       sec=0.6*rem
       hh,cc=improve_cont(best_h,n,sec,rng,3.0,0.03)
       if cc < best_c: update
       rem=hard_end-time.time()
       if rem > 12:
          hh,cc=improve_cont(best_h,n,rem-5.0,rng,0.25,0.005)
          if cc < best_c: update
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c=final_c(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c,n

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def project_feasible(v, n):
    target = n / 2.0
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(8):
        diff = target - v.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            return v
        if diff > 0:
            mask = v < 1.0 - 1e-15
        else:
            mask = v > 1e-15
        if not mask.any():
            break
        v[mask] += diff / mask.sum()
        v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)

    if abs(v.sum() - target) > 1e-11:
        lo, hi = -1.0, 1.0
        for _ in range(40):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            s = np.clip(v + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
            if s < target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        v = np.clip(v + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - v.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        free = (v > 1e-12) & (v < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if free.any():
            v[free] += diff / free.sum()
            v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
    return v


def S_of(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full").astype(float)


def c_of(S, n):
    return float(np.max(S) * (2.0 / n))


def final_c(h, n):
    return c_of(S_of(h, n), n)


def smooth_info(S, n, tau, active_tol):
    m = float(S.max())
    dx = 2.0 / n
    if active_tol is not None:
        w = (S >= m - active_tol).astype(float)
        s = w.sum()
        if s == 0.0:
            w = np.zeros_like(S)
            w[int(np.argmax(S))] = 1.0
        else:
            w /= s
        return w, m * dx

    sh = (S - m) / tau
    ex = np.exp(sh)
    den = ex.sum()
    if den <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(S)
        w[int(np.argmax(S))] = 1.0
        return w, m * dx
    w = ex / den
    return w, (m + tau * math.log(den)) * dx


def grad_weights(h, n, w):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    G = np.zeros(n)
    center = n - 1
    nz = np.nonzero(w > 1e-10)[0]
    for k in nz:
        wt = float(w[k]) * dx
        if wt <= 0.0:
            continue
        d = center - int(k)
        if d == 0:
            G += wt * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif d > 0:
            L = n - d
            G[:L] += wt * (1.0 - h[d:])
            G[d:] -= wt * h[:L]
        else:
            e = -d
            G[e:] += wt * (1.0 - h[:e])
            G[:e] -= wt * h[e:]
    return G


def pair_move(h, n, rng):
    S = S_of(h, n)
    m = float(S.max())
    cur = c_of(S, n)
    tol = 0.35
    act = (S >= m - tol)
    cnt = float(act.sum())
    if cnt <= 0.0:
        act = np.zeros_like(S)
        act[int(np.argmax(S))] = 1.0
    else:
        act = act / cnt
    G = grad_weights(h, n, act)
    best_c = cur
    best_h = None

    dirs = [(int(np.argmin(G)), int(np.argmax(G))), (int(np.argmax(G)), int(np.argmin(G)))]
    for inc, dec in dirs:
        if inc == dec:
            continue
        tmax = min(1.0 - h[inc], h[dec])
        if tmax <= 1e-9:
            continue
        fractions = (0.5, 0.33, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.005)
        for fr in fractions:
            t = fr * tmax
            nh = h.copy()
            nh[inc] += t
            nh[dec] -= t
            c = final_c(nh, n)
            if c < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c
                best_h = nh.copy()
    if best_h is None:
        return None
    return project_feasible(best_h, n), best_c


def improve_cont(start, n, seconds, rng, tau_start=6.0, tau_min=0.02):
    if seconds <= 0.0:
        h = project_feasible(start, n)
        return h, final_c(h, n)

    end = time.time() + float(seconds)
    h = project_feasible(start, n)
    S = S_of(h, n)
    cur = c_of(S, n)
    best = cur
    best_h = h.copy()

    tau = float(tau_start)
    stall = 0
    accepts = 0

    while time.time() < end:
        S = S_of(h, n)
        cur = c_of(S, n)
        if cur < best - 1e-13:
            best = cur
            best_h = h.copy()
        if cur > best + 0.02:
            h = best_h.copy()
            S = S_of(h, n)
            cur = best

        active = None
        if tau < 0.08:
            active = 0.25 if tau >= 0.03 else 0.15
            w, f = smooth_info(S, n, tau, active)
        else:
            w, f = smooth_info(S, n, tau, None)

        G = grad_weights(h, n, w)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(G))
        accepted = False

        if gn > 1e-11:
            base = min(1.0, 0.25 / gn, 1.0e6)
            for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25):
                a = base * mult
                if a <= 0.0:
                    continue
                cand = project_feasible(h - a * G, n)
                Sc = S_of(cand, n)
                cc = c_of(Sc, n)
                if cc < cur - 1e-12:
                    h = cand
                    cur = cc
                    accepted = True
                    if cc < best - 1e-13:
                        best = cc
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        stall = 0
                    break
                if active is None and cc < max(cur, best) + 0.01:
                    _, fc = smooth_info(Sc, n, tau, None)
                    if fc < f - 1e-10:
                        h = cand
                        cur = cc
                        accepted = True
                        if cc < best - 1e-13:
                            best = cc
                            best_h = h.copy()
                            stall = 0
                        break

        if accepted:
            accepts += 1
            if accepts % 80 == 0:
                tau = max(float(tau_min), tau * 0.97)
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall == 5:
                tau = max(float(tau_min), tau * 0.6)
            if stall >= 40 and stall % 10 == 0:
                pm = pair_move(best_h, n, rng)
                if pm is not None and pm[1] < best - 1e-13:
                    best = pm[1]
                    best_h = project_feasible(pm[0], n)
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    cur = best
                    stall = 0
            if stall >= 60:
                sigma = 0.008 + 0.012 * min(1.0, max(tau, 0.0) / 3.0)
                h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), n)
                cur = final_c(h, n)
                if cur > best + 0.02:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    cur = best
                stall = 0
                tau = max(tau, 0.5)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    return best_h, min(best, final_c(best_h, n))


def binary_round(h, n):
    b = np.zeros(n)
    idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    b[idx[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    return b


def binary_local(start, n, seconds, rng):
    if seconds <= 0.0:
        b = binary_round(start, n)
        return b, final_c(b, n)

    end = time.time() + float(seconds)
    b = binary_round(start, n)
    S = S_of(b, n)
    cur = c_of(S, n)
    best = cur
    bb = b.copy()
    dx = 2.0 / n
    center = n - 1
    stall = 0

    while time.time() < end:
        S = S_of(b, n)
        m = float(S.max())
        cur = m * dx
        if cur < best - 1e-12:
            best = cur
            bb = b.copy()
            stall = 0

        active = np.where(S >= m - 1.0)[0]
        cand = []
        seen = set()

        for k in active:
            d = center - int(k)
            if d > 0:
                L = n - d
                if L <= 0:
                    continue
                mask = (b[:L] == 1.0) & (b[d:] == 0.0)
                pts = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
                for p in pts[:5]:
                    z = d + int(p)
                    o = int(p)
                    if b[z] == 0.0 and b[o] == 1.0:
                        pr = (z, o)
                        if pr not in seen:
                            seen.add(pr)
                            cand.append(pr)
                            if len(cand) >= 90:
                                break
            elif d < 0:
                e = -d
                L = n - e
                if L <= 0:
                    continue
                mask = (b[e:] == 1.0) & (b[:e] == 0.0)
                pts = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
                for p in pts[:5]:
                    z = e + int(p)
                    o = int(p)
                    if b[z] == 0.0 and b[o] == 1.0:
                        pr = (z, o)
                        if pr not in seen:
                            seen.add(pr)
                            cand.append(pr)
                            if len(cand) >= 90:
                                break
            if len(cand) >= 90:
                break

        ones = np.where(b == 1.0)[0]
        zeros = np.where(b == 0.0)[0]
        for _ in range(30):
            if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
                break
            z = int(rng.choice(zeros))
            o = int(rng.choice(ones))
            pr = (z, o)
            if pr not in seen:
                seen.add(pr)
                cand.append(pr)

        improved = False
        if cand:
            order = rng.permutation(len(cand))
            for idx in order:
                z, o = cand[idx]
                nb = b.copy()
                nb[z] = 1.0
                nb[o] = 0.0
                c = final_c(nb, n)
                if c < cur - 1e-12:
                    b = nb
                    cur = c
                    improved = True
                    break

        if improved:
            continue

        stall += 1
        if stall > 40:
            temp = 0.012
            esc = False
            for _ in range(25):
                if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
                    break
                z = int(rng.choice(zeros))
                o = int(rng.choice(ones))
                nb = b.copy()
                nb[z] = 1.0
                nb[o] = 0.0
                c = final_c(nb, n)
                if c < best + temp:
                    b = nb
                    cur = c
                    esc = True
                    break
            if not esc and stall > 120:
                b = bb.copy()
                cur = best
                stall = 0

    bb = project_feasible(bb, n)
    return bb, min(best, final_c(bb, n))


def add_candidate(collection, h, n, counter):
    hh = project_feasible(h, n)
    cc = final_c(hh, n)
    collection.append((cc, counter, hh))
    return counter + 1


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    hard_end = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        n = 250
        h0 = 0.5 * np.ones(n)

    if n < 2:
        n = 250
        h0 = 0.5 * np.ones(n)

    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = final_c(h0, n)

    collection = []
    ctr = 0
    ctr = add_candidate(collection, h0, n, ctr)

    for sig in (0.01, 0.03, 0.08):
        ctr = add_candidate(collection, h0 + rng.normal(0.0, sig, n), n, ctr)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    c1 = np.cos(np.pi * x)
    c2 = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)

    for a1 in np.linspace(-0.5, 0.5, 11):
        for a2 in np.linspace(-0.3, 0.3, 7):
            hh = 0.5 + a1 * c1 + a2 * c2
            ctr = add_candidate(collection, hh, n, ctr)

    M = 6
    scales = np.array([0.25, 0.15, 0.08, 0.05, 0.03, 0.02])
    B = np.empty((n, M))
    for j in range(M):
        B[:, j] = np.cos((j + 1) * np.pi * x)

    for _ in range(40):
        coeff = rng.normal(0.0, scales[:M])
        hh = 0.5 + B.dot(coeff)
        ctr = add_candidate(collection, hh, n, ctr)

    kernel = np.array([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 2.0, 1.0])
    kernel /= 9.0
    for _ in range(6):
        hh = 0.5 + np.convolve(kernel, rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, n), mode="same")
        ctr = add_candidate(collection, hh, n, ctr)

    collection.sort()
    cands = []
    for _cv, _id, hh in collection:
        cands.append(hh)
        if len(cands) >= 6:
            break

    usable = hard_end - time.time()
    if usable < 25.0 or not cands:
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
        return best_h, final_c(best_h, n), n

    weights = np.ones(min(5, len(cands)))
    weights[0] = 1.4
    if len(weights) > 1:
        weights[1] = 0.9
    if len(weights) > 2:
        weights[2] = 0.7
    if len(weights) > 3:
        weights[3] = 0.5
    if len(weights) > 4:
        weights[4] = 0.4
    wsum = float(weights.sum())
    nonfinal = 0.60 * usable

    for idx in range(len(weights)):
        if time.time() >= hard_end - 0.34 * usable:
            break
        sec = nonfinal * (weights[idx] / wsum)
        if sec < 8.0:
            sec = 8.0
        hh, cc = improve_cont(cands[idx], n, sec, rng, 6.0, 0.02)
        if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hh

    rem = hard_end - time.time()

    if n % 2 == 0 and rem > 150.0:
        sec = min(70.0, rem - 80.0)
        if sec > 10.0:
            bb, cb = binary_local(best_h, n, sec, rng)
            if cb < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = cb
                best_h = project_feasible(bb, n)
            rem = hard_end - time.time()

    if rem > 25.0:
        sec = 0.60 * rem
        hh, cc = improve_cont(best_h, n, sec, rng, 3.0, 0.03)
        if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hh

        rem = hard_end - time.time()
        if rem > 12.0:
            hh, cc = improve_cont(best_h, n, rem - 5.0, rng, 0.25, 0.005)
            if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = hh

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = final_c(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c, n
```
